### [113] [A degenerate Whittaker criterion for $\mathrm GL_{2n}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01598)
*Taiwang Deng*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究GL_{2n}(F)上不可约容许表示π的广义Whittaker模型消失性，证明了Gomez-Gourevitch-Sahi关于扭曲Jacquet模π_{N,ψ}消失性的轨道理论判据等价于Zelevinsky对偶中包含长度至少n+1的线段。回应了Prasad关于π_{N,ψ}消失性与伴随L-函数L(s,π×π^∨)极点不等式关系的猜想，证明了消失性蕴含极点不等式，但通过GL_4(F)反例说明逆向不成立。对广义Steinberg分量给出了精确计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应D. Prasad提出的猜想：关于GL_{2n}(F)上不可约容许表示的广义Whittaker模型消失性与伴随L-函数L(s,π×π^∨)极点不等式之间的关系。

Method: 将Gomez-Gourevitch-Sahi的轨道理论判据（波前集）翻译为Langlands-Zelevinsky数据语言，利用Zelevinsky对偶中线段长度条件刻画π_{N,ψ}的消失性，并通过显式反例和广义Steinberg分量的计算验证猜想。

Result: 证明了π_{N,ψ}=0当且仅当Zelevinsky对偶m^t包含长度≥n+1的线段；消失性蕴含Prasad猜想的极点不等式，但逆向不成立（GL_4(F)反例）；对GL_6(F)的主序列中32个广义Steinberg分量，恰好3个违反逆向方向。

Conclusion: Prasad猜想中消失性⇒极点不等式成立，但逆向不成立，需修正。

Abstract: Let $F$ be a non-Archimedean local field. Let $N$ be the unipotent radical of the standard parabolic subgroup of $\mathrm GL_{2n}(F)$ of type $(n,n)$ with fixed nondegenerate additive character $ψ$. For an irreducible admissible representation $π$ of $\mathrm GL_{2n}(F)$, a theorem due to Gomez--Gourevitch--Sahi on generalized Whittaker models gives a criterion for the vanishing of the twisted Jacquet module $π_{N,ψ}$ in terms of the wave-front set. We translate this orbit-theoretic answer into Langlands--Zelevinsky data: if $π=L(\mathfrak m)$, then $π_{N,ψ}=0$ if and only if the Zelevinsky dual $\mathfrak m^{\mathrm t}$ contains a segment of length at least $n+1$.
  We do this in response to a conjecture proposed by D.Prasad about the vanishing of $π_{N,ψ}$ in terms of the adjoint $L$-function $L(s,π\timesπ^\vee)$. We prove that, for every irreducible representation $π$, vanishing of $π_{N,ψ}$ implies the pole inequalities predicted by D.Prasad. However, we show that the converse implication is false by an explicit counterexample for $\mathrm GL_4(F)$.
  For the generalized Steinberg constituents $v_{P_β}^G$ of the principal series containing the trivial representation, we make an explicit calculation of when $π_{N,ψ}$ is zero. In particular, for $\mathrm GL_6(F)$, exactly three of the $32$ constituents of such a principal series violate the converse direction of the conjecture proposed by D.Prasad.

</details>


### [114] [Euler characters for general linear Lie superalgebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01782)
*A. N. Sergeev*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了李超代数 $\frak{gl}(m,n)$ 的欧拉特征标，采用了与 Gorelik 和 Heidersdors 不同的方法，并以不同术语表述结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究（Gorelik 和 Heidersdors）探讨了 $\frak{gl}(m,n)$ 和 $\frak{osp}(m,2n)$ 的欧拉特征标，但本文旨在为 $\frak{gl}(m,n)$ 提供一种新的研究视角和方法。

Method: 采用与 Gorelik 和 Heidersdors 不同的方法来研究李超代数 $\frak{gl}(m,n)$ 的欧拉特征标。

Result: 得到了关于 $\frak{gl}(m,n)$ 欧拉特征标的结果，但以不同术语进行表述。

Conclusion: 为 $\frak{gl}(m,n)$ 的欧拉特征标提供了另一种研究途径和表述方式。

Abstract: M. Gorelik and Th. Heidersdors in the papers \cite{GH} investigated Euler characters for Lie superalgebra $\frak{gl}(m,n)$ and $\frak{osp}(m.2n)$. In the present paper we also investigate Euler characters for Lie superalgebra $\frak{gl}(m,n)$ but we use a different approach and our results are formulated in different terms.

</details>


### [115] [Simple restricted modules over the deformative Schrödinger-Virasoro algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01944)
*Haibo Chen,Yongtao Liu,Xiaoqing Yue*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文对形变Schrödinger-Virasoro代数$\mathcal{G}_{λ,μ}$上的简单限制模进行了完整分类，包括最高权模和Whittaker模，并将其推广到顶点代数上的弱$V(c)$-模，统一了Schrödinger-Virasoro代数和形变$\mathfrak{bms}_3$代数的表示理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类形变Schrödinger-Virasoro代数$\mathcal{G}_{λ,μ}$上的简单限制模，并改进已有结果（[5,Theorem 3.4]和[6,Theorem 2]）。

Method: 通过从正部商代数诱导简单模，系统构造了包括最高权模和Whittaker模在内的限制模；证明满足某些单射条件的简单限制模同构于此类诱导模。

Result: 给出了对于某些$λ,μ\in\mathbb{C}$的简单限制模的完整分类，并推广得到了顶点代数上相关的简单弱$V(c)$-模。

Conclusion: 该工作有效分类并推广了形变Schrödinger-Virasoro代数族的表示理论，统一了Schrödinger-Virasoro代数和形变$\mathfrak{bms}_3$代数的特殊情况。

Abstract: This paper investigates simple restricted modules over the deformed Schrödinger-Virasoro algebra $\mathcal{G}_{λ,μ}$, which gives a complete classification of them for some $λ,μ\in\mathbb{C}$. More precisely, we provide a systematic construction of these modules, including highest weight modules and Whittaker modules, by inducing simple modules from the positive part's quotient algebras. We prove that any simple restricted $\mathcal{G}_{λ,μ}$-module satisfying certain injective conditions is isomorphic to such an induced module. As an application, we obtain some simple weak $V(c)$-modules over vertex algebras associated to $\mathcal{G}_{λ,μ}$ for some $λ,μ\in\mathbb{C}$. Note that our results include the Schrödinger-Virasoro algebra and the deformed $\mathfrak{bms}_3$ algebra as special cases, thereby improving upon some of the previously reported results of [5,Theorem 3.4] and [6,Theorem 2]. This work effectively classifies and generalizes the representation theory of the deformed family.

</details>


### [116] [Feigin-Semikhatov duality at the critical level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02155)
*Thomas Creutzig,Xuanzhong Dai,Bailin Song*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Feigin-Semikhatov对偶在临界/大水平极限下的表现，描述了临界水平下的子正则W-代数作为大水平极限主W-超代数与格点VOA的轨形，构建了函子并证明了分块等价性，揭示了权模范畴中主块的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Feigin-Semikhatov对偶在临界/大水平极限下的具体实现，理解两类顶点代数之间的深层联系。

Method: 将临界水平下的中心less子正则W-代数描述为大水平极限下主W-超代数与格点VOA的轨形；构建函子；研究主块结构。

Result: 获得了分块范畴等价性；揭示了权模范畴（比常见的下界模范畴更大）中子正则W-代数主块的结构。

Conclusion: 在临界/大水平极限下，Feigin-Semikhatov对偶给出了权模范畴中分块等价，并阐明了相关顶点代数主块的结构。

Abstract: The Feigin-Semikhatov duality asserts that the Heisenberg cosets of the subregular $W$-algebra of $\mathfrak{sl}_n$ at level $k$ and the one of the principal $W$-superalgebra of $\mathfrak{sl}_{n|1}$ at level $\ell$ coincide when the levels satisfy the Feigin-Frenkel relation $(k+n)(\ell+n-1)=1$. A similar duality holds between the subregular $W$-algebra of $\mathfrak{so}_{2n+1}$ and the principal $W$-superalgebra of $\mathfrak{osp}_{2|2n}$.
  We study these dualities in the critical/large level limit.
  We describe the centerless subregular $W$-algebra at the critical level as an orbifold of the large level limit of the principal $W$-superalgebra times a lattice VOA. Our construction yields a functor between certain categories of the two involved vertex algebras. We show that in this set-up one in fact gets block-wise equivalences of categories. Studying the principal block of the large level limit of the principal $W$-superalgebra then gives us the structure of the principal blocks of the subregular $W$-algebras in the category of weight modules (which is much larger than the more common category of lower bounded modules).

</details>


### [117] [Part bounds for the Sylow permutation characters of $S_n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02433)
*Lorenzo Vanzi*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 研究了对称群在素数2下的Sylow置换特征，并证明了其组成部分对应划分的划分数的新界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称群的Sylow置换特征在表示论和组合学中具有重要地位，但对其组成部分对应的划分的划分数界限尚不清楚，需要进一步研究。

Method: 通过对对称群在素数2下的Sylow子群及其置换特征进行分析，结合表示论和划分组合性质，推导出新的界限。

Result: 得到了关于划分数的新的上界或下界结果。

Conclusion: 为对称群Sylow置换特征的结构提供了更精确的组合描述，推动了相关领域的研究进展。

Abstract: We study the Sylow permutation character of the symmetric group at the prime 2 and prove some new bounds on the number of parts of partitions corresponding to its constituents.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [A homotopy coherent Pontryagin-Thom isomorphism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01482)
*Kenneth Blakey,Liam Keenan*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该工作构造了一个以（结构化）几何配边为单位的、带转移的齐次不变层稳定∞-范畴，并证明了单位层的自同态环可典范地等同于相应的E_∞-Thom环谱，从而给出了Pontryagin-Thom同构的E_∞提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Pontryagin-Thom同构断言（结构化）几何配边所给出的乘法上同调理论等价于由关联Thom谱决定的上同调理论，但该同构仅在谱水平上成立，缺乏更高阶的E_∞结构相容性。作者希望在该同构中加入E_∞环结构信息，即提供一个E_∞层次的提升。

Method: 构造了一个带转移的齐次不变层（homotopy invariant sheaves with transfers）的对称幺半稳定∞-范畴，并证明其单位对象恰好是（结构化）几何配边；然后通过计算该单位层的自同态环，将其与关联的E_∞-Thom环谱典范等同。

Result: 成功构造了一个以几何配边为单位的对称幺半稳定∞-范畴，并建立了单位层自同态环与E_∞-Thom环谱之间的典范同构，即实现了Pontryagin-Thom同构的E_∞提升。

Conclusion: 本文在E_∞环谱的层面上推广了经典Pontryagin-Thom同构，为配边理论与Thom谱之间的等价提供了更高阶的乘法结构相容性。

Abstract: Classically, the Pontryagin-Thom isomorphism asserts that the multiplicative cohomology theory given by (structured) geometric cobordism is isomorphic to the cohomology theory determined by an associated Thom spectrum. We construct a presentably symmetric monoidal stable $\infty$-category of homotopy invariant sheaves with transfers on smooth manifolds whose unit is precisely (structured) geometric cobordism. We show the endomorphism ring of the unit sheaf can be canonically identified with the associated $\mathbb{E}_\infty$-Thom ring spectrum, i.e., we provide an $\mathbb{E}_\infty$-lift of the Pontryagin-Thom isomorphism.

</details>


### [119] [Reduced characteristic number criteria for equivariant bordism of $T^k$- and $(\mathbb{Z}_2)^k$-manifolds with isolated fixed points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01889)
*Runze Chen,Zhi Lü,Leqi Yang*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文为两类具有孤立不动点的等变流形（酉T^k-流形和光滑(Z_2)^k-流形）建立了简化的等变配边判别准则。对于酉T^k-流形，仅需一个等变陈类多项式即可判定；并引入了最小区分度，推演出关于维数与欧拉示性数的两个关键不等式，部分验证了Kosniowski猜想及其环面推广。对于光滑(Z_2)^k-流形，仅依赖最高阶等变Stiefel-Whitney类的幂次即可完成判别，大幅降低了计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典等变配边理论需要计算完整的等变示性数集合才能判定等变流形是否等变配边，计算量巨大。本文旨在为两类具有孤立不动点的等变流形建立显著简化的配边判别条件，并由此研究Kosniowski猜想中的下界问题。

Method: 对于酉T^k-流形，构造单个等变陈类多项式作为配边判别准则；引入最小区分度的概念，推导其与维数和欧拉示性数的不等式关系。对于光滑(Z_2)^k-流形，利用最高阶等变Stiefel-Whitney类的幂次建立更简洁的判别条件。

Result: 建立了酉T^k-流形的基于单个等变陈类多项式的配边判别准则；得到了最小区分度的两个关键不等式，部分验证了Kosniowski猜想；在维数dim M=2k情形下给出了该猜想环面推广的另一种证明；建立了光滑(Z_2)^k-流形基于最高阶等变Stiefel-Whitney类幂次的更简洁配边判别准则。

Conclusion: 本文大幅简化了具有孤立不动点的等变配边理论的计算复杂度，推进了Kosniowski猜想的理解，并为该猜想在环面情形的推广提供了新证明。新的判别准则在实际计算中具有显著优势。

Abstract: Classical equivariant bordism theories require computing the full collection of equivariant characteristic numbers to detect whether an equivariant manifold bounds equivariantly or not. This paper establishes simplified equivariant bordism characterizations for two families of equivariant manifolds with isolated fixed points: unitary $T^k$-manifolds and closed smooth $(\mathbb{Z}_2)^k$-manifolds. For any unitary $T^k$-manifold $M$ with isolated fixed points, we establish an equivariant unitary bordism criterion built entirely from a single polynomial of equivariant Chern classes. We further introduce the minimal distinguishing degree and obtain two key inequalities that capture the interplay between $\dim M$ and the Euler characteristic $χ(M)$ through this minimal distinguishing degree. These inequalities settle the existence problem of a linear lower bound for $χ(M)$ within the framework of Kosniowski's conjecture and partially verify the conjecture under natural admissible assumptions. We also provide an alternative proof settling the toric generalization of Kosniowski's conjecture when $\dim M=2k$. By contrast, for a closed smooth $(\mathbb{Z}_2)^k$-manifold with isolated fixed points, we derive a more concise equivariant bordism criterion relying solely on the powers of the top equivariant Stiefel-Whitney class. Our new criteria substantially reduce computational demands.

</details>


### [120] [Equivariant twisted $R$-algebras via Thom spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02060)
*Samik Basu,Abhinandan Das*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究在 $C_2$-交换环谱 $R$ 上构造 twisted $R$-代数，特别是当 $R$ 为偶数实交换环谱时，通过 Thom 谱构造各种商代数，并计算实拓扑 Hochschild 同调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在等变同伦论中，$C_2$-交换环谱上的 twisted 代数结构是理解实代数拓扑（如实 K-理论、实配边理论）的重要工具。本文旨在系统构造这类 twisted 代数，并发展其同调计算工具。

Method: 1. 将商对象构造为从 $S^1$ 和 $U(n)$ 上适当的 $C_2$-作用出发的 Thom 谱。2. 利用 Thom 谱的公式简化实拓扑 Hochschild 同调 (THH) 的计算。3. 通过 $gl_1K\mathbb{R}$ 的单位谱分裂（类比经典 K-理论单位谱分裂）辅助计算。

Result: 给出了 $K\mathbb{R}$ 上的 twisted $K\mathbb{R}$-代数结构；构造了实配边谱 $M\mathbb{R}$ 的商 $M\mathbb{R}/(2,x_1,\dots,x_{n-1})$；将实 2-周期 Morava K-理论作为实 Morava E-理论谱上的模；计算了 $K\mathbb{R}/2$ 的实拓扑 Hochschild 同调。

Conclusion: twisted $R$-代数框架统一了 $K\mathbb{R}$、实配边谱和实 Morava 理论等对象的商结构，且 Thom 谱方法有效计算了实 THH，为等变同调代数提供了新工具。

Abstract: For a $C_2$-commutative ring spectrum $R$, a twisted $R$-algebra is an $R$-module with a multiplication whose order is switched by the $C_2$-action. In this paper, we construct various quotients of $R$ as twisted $R$-algebras, when $R$ is an even real commutative ring spectrum. These are constructed as Thom spectra of maps out of suitable $C_2$-actions on $S^1$ and $U(n)$. One such example is given by $K\mathbb{R}$ which is endowed with a twisted $K\mathbb{R}$-algebra structure. Other examples include quotients such as $M\mathbb{R}/(2,x_1,\dots, x_{n-1})$ over the real bordism spectrum $M\mathbb{R}$, and the real $2$-periodic Morava $K$-theories as modules over the real Morava $E$-theory spectra. In the context of twisted $R$-algebras, one may consider the real topological Hochschild homology, and for Thom spectra, one has a nice formula again as a Thom spectrum. We use this to obtain computations for the real topological Hochschild homology of $K\mathbb{R}/2$ as a twisted $K\mathbb{R}$-algebra. The computation also involves a splitting of the units spectrum $gl_1K\mathbb{R}$, which is an analogue of the classical splitting of the units of $K$-theory.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [Bilinear control of age--space structured populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01347)
*Jiguang Yu,Louis Shuo Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究了具有更新边界条件和内源监测反馈的非局部年龄-空间结构种群方程的约束双线性最优控制问题。通过特征线温和公式建立了闭环适定性和Frechet可微性，推导了约化及反馈校正的伴随方程，证明了反馈导数为低秩扰动，并给出了Volterra核情形下伴随的显式预解表示，最后推导了一阶最优性条件并将切换函数分解为约化分量和反馈诱导分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局部年龄-空间结构种群方程是描述具有年龄结构和空间扩散的种群动态的重要模型，但现有的最优控制理论主要针对开环系统，缺乏对反馈（尤其是内源监测反馈）作用下的闭环控制问题的系统分析。本文旨在建立这类问题在约束双线性控制下的理论框架。

Method: 采用特征线温和公式（而非标准的Lions-Magenes论证）处理闭环系统的适定性；利用Frechet导数分析控制到状态映射的可微性；通过识别反馈导数为低秩扰动来简化伴随方程的结构；在Volterra核假设下分析转移算子的拟幂零性以获取显式预解表示。

Result: 建立了闭环系统的适定性和Frechet可微性；推导了约化伴随方程和反馈校正伴随方程；证明了反馈导数项表现为低秩扰动形式ℓ_{\bar y,\bar u}(p)(t)χ(a,x)；在Volterra核条件下转移算子为拟幂零，从而得到伴随的显式表示；推导了包含约化和反馈两部分的一阶最优性条件。

Conclusion: 本文为具有内源监测反馈的非局部年龄-空间结构种群方程的双线性最优控制提供了完整的理论框架，所发展的特征线温和公式方法和反馈校正伴随方程推导策略可推广至其他具有闭环反馈机制的分布参数系统的最优控制问题。

Abstract: We study constrained bilinear optimal control for nonlocal age--space structured population equations with renewal boundary conditions and endogenous surveillance feedback. The control acts as a coefficient in a mixed transport--diffusion equation, while a scalar observable generated by the state enters both the interior dynamics and the renewal law. This produces a nonlinear closed-loop control-to-state map and a feedback-dependent adjoint system. Using a characteristic mild formulation rather than a standard Lions--Magenes argument, we establish closed-loop well-posedness and Frechet differentiability. We then derive the reduced and feedback-corrected adjoint equations. The feedback derivative is identified as a low-rank perturbation $\ell_{\bar y,\bar u}(p)(t)χ(a,x)$; in the Volterra-kernel regime, the associated transfer operator is quasinilpotent, yielding an explicit resolvent representation of the adjoint. Finally, we prove first-order optimality conditions and decompose the switching function into reduced and feedback-induced components.

</details>


### [122] [Boundary-Aware Quantization: Finite-Scale Decision Geometry of Neural Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01478)
*O. M. Kiselev*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统量化了量化导致的决策边界变化，提出了多种边界变化度量指标，并在多个基准上验证了校准边界Jaccard系数对留出边界变化的强预测能力（r=0.947–0.994）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准量化评估仅关注精度等宏观指标，忽略了量化对模型决策边界的微观影响。本文旨在系统量化和理解量化导致的决策边界重配置。

Method: 提出多种边界变化度量：局部logit边缘半径、一阶边界位移、法向变化、切片边界Jaccard距离、网格预测变化、多类交点计数、低边缘边界带翻转。在digits、MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10上评估PTQ-W及优化舍入变体，并引入校准到测试停止规则和固定位边界间隙舍入项。

Result: 8位权重量化在digits上保留所有测试标签；4位时精度0.9733，边界Jaccard升至0.970。校准-测试停止将digits留出翻转率从0.0094降至0.0022，边界Jaccard从0.825降至0.524。边界感知停止在CIFAR-10子集上实现8位翻转率0.0083、边界Jaccard 0.048。6位PTQ-W在CIFAR-10上改变5.3%留出决策和24.5%低边缘边界带决策。固定位边界间隙舍入项在4位将边界Jaccard从0.457降至0.435。校准边界Jaccard与留出边界Jaccard相关性达r=0.947–0.994。

Conclusion: 决策边界度量揭示了精度无法捕获的量化效应；校准边界Jaccard可作为量化边界变化的可靠代理指标，指导量化精度与边界保真度的权衡。

Abstract: We measured quantization-induced decision-boundary changes using local logit-margin radii, first-order boundary displacement, normal variation, slice-boundary Jaccard distance, grid prediction changes, multiclass junction counts, and low-margin boundary-band flips. On the digits benchmark, 8-bit weight quantization preserved all test labels while producing boundary-mask Jaccard \(0.428\) on the PCA slice; at 4 bits, accuracy remained \(0.9733\), while boundary Jaccard rose to \(0.970\) and median local boundary shift reached \(0.0290\). Interpolation between adjacent quantization levels localized the visible reconfigurations at multiclass junctions, with 12, 34, and 17 triple-junction cells in the selected transitions. Calibration-to-test stopping reduced the digits held-out flip rate from \(0.0094\) to \(0.0022\) and boundary Jaccard from \(0.825\) to \(0.524\); the same stopping rule also reduced flips on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST. On official CIFAR-10 subsets, PTQ-W selected by accuracy gave 6-bit flip \(0.0367\) and boundary Jaccard \(0.184\), whereas boundary-aware stopping selected 8-bit flip \(0.0083\) and boundary Jaccard \(0.048\). On full CIFAR-10 with three seeds, 6-bit PTQ-W lost \(0.0029\) accuracy relative to float, changed \(5.3\%\) of held-out decisions, and changed \(24.5\%\) of low-margin boundary-band decisions. A fixed-bit boundary-gap rounding term changed the trade-off at 4 bits by reducing boundary Jaccard from \(0.457\) to \(0.435\) and boundary-band pair-order flip from \(0.3600\) to \(0.3558\), with an accuracy trade-off; the 3-bit stress test exposed the tuning limit of this surrogate. Calibration boundary Jaccard predicted held-out boundary Jaccard across PTQ-W and optimized rounding variants with \(r=0.947\)--\(0.994\).

</details>


### [123] [Admission and Assortment Optimization for Multi-size Automated Parcel Lockers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01489)
*Carlos Aníbal Suárez,Antoine Deza,Tal Raviv*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了多尺寸自动包裹柜的准入控制和容量设计问题，提出了基于马尔可夫决策过程的模型，分析了始终接受策略的最优性条件，并通过精确算法和局部搜索方法求解了柜体组合优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动包裹柜中不同尺寸包裹与柜格的匹配存在效率与阻塞成本之间的权衡——小包裹占用大柜格可能阻碍后续大包裹的投递，而大包裹被拒成本更高，因此需要优化准入控制与容量配置以最小化总成本。

Method: 将准入问题建模为有限状态、无限时域平均成本马尔可夫决策过程，使用相对值迭代精确求解小规模实例；分析了始终接受策略的最优性充分条件；对于柜体组合问题，提出了边界枚举精确算法和交换邻域局部搜索启发式方法。

Result: 在两尺寸、三尺寸和四尺寸实验中，始终接受策略在快速取件场景下最优，在长持有时间下接近最优且差距可忽略；局部搜索方法在所有可精确验证的实例中均找到认证最优解，并可作为启发式方法扩展至更大系统。

Conclusion: 始终接受策略在多数实际场景中表现优异，局部搜索方法在柜体组合优化中兼具精确性和可扩展性，为自动包裹柜的实际运营与设计提供了理论依据和实用算法。

Abstract: We study admission control and capacity design for automated parcel lockers with multiple parcel and locker sizes. A smaller parcel can use a larger locker, but doing so may block a future larger parcel whose rejection is more costly. We formulate the admission problem as a finite-state, infinite-horizon average-cost Markov decision process and solve small instances exactly by relative value iteration. We analyze the always-accept (AA) policy, which admits every feasible parcel into the remaining compatible capacity, and give a sufficient condition for its optimality. Across two-, three-, and four-size experiments, AA is optimal in fast-pickup regimes and nearly optimal when holding times are longer; observed optimality gaps are negligible even when AA is not optimal. We then study the locker-assortment problem, which minimizes facility cost plus optimal expected rejection cost. We give an exact bound-and-enumerate algorithm for moderate-size instances. Although the objective is not discrete convex, exchange-neighborhood local search finds the certified optimum in every instance for which exact certification is computationally tractable, and it scales as a heuristic to larger systems.

</details>


### [124] [Flexible and Reliable Network Design for Emerging Transportation Services: Multi-stage Stochastic Programming Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01524)
*Riki Kawase,Koki Satsukawa,Toru Seo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向灵活可靠网络设计问题（FR-NDPs）的通用框架，将战略投资决策与运营控制整合为风险规避的多阶段随机规划问题，并通过随机对偶动态规划（SDDP）求解。在纽约曼哈顿中城的共享自动驾驶汽车（SAV）容量扩展和SAV-BRT路线设计实验中，发现灵活性与可靠性互补，风险规避型规划者倾向于减少早期投资以保留适应性，并避免投资运营灵活性较低的交通模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴交通服务（如共享自动驾驶汽车系统）面临历史数据稀缺和技术发展不确定性的挑战，现有网络设计方法难以同时兼顾投资灵活性和运营可靠性。

Method: 将FR-NDPs建模为风险规避的多阶段随机规划问题，采用随机对偶动态规划（SDDP）求解，并建立了战略与运营子问题收敛到全局最优的充分条件。

Result: 曼哈顿中城网络实验表明：(i) 灵活性与可靠性互补；(ii) 灵活的投资规划实现动态风险对冲，风险规避型规划者减少早期投资；(iii) 风险规避型规划者避免投资运营灵活性较低的交通模式。

Conclusion: 灵活性和可靠性在应对不确定场景中具有互补作用，该框架能有效指导不确定性下的交通基础设施投资决策。

Abstract: This paper proposes a general framework for flexible and reliable network design problems (FR-NDPs). The framework enables planners to change infrastructure investments in response to realized uncertainties, while ensuring desired levels of reliability. Motivated by emerging transportation services such as shared autonomous vehicle (SAV) systems, where historical data are scarce and technological developments uncertain, FR-NDPs integrate strategic investment decisions with operational control. We formulate the FR-NDPs as risk-averse multi-stage stochastic problems to be solvable by stochastic dual dynamic programming (SDDP) and establish sufficient conditions under which strategic and operational subproblems converge to the global optimum. We illustrate applications to SAV capacity expansion and integrated SAV-BRT (Bus Rapid Transit) route design, and numerical experiments on a Midtown Manhattan network highlight three key findings: (i) flexibility and reliability act complementarily to hedge against severe scenarios while mitigating the loss of expected performance; (ii) flexibility in investment planning allows dynamic risk hedging, with risk-averse planners reducing early-stage investments to preserve adaptability; and (iii) differences in operational flexibility between SAV and BRT systems are reflected in strategic decisions, with risk-averse planners tending to refrain investment in transport modes with lower operational flexibility.

</details>


### [125] [Mean Field Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01525)
*René Carmona,Mathieu Laurière*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该专著系统介绍了平均场强化学习，通过大规模群体随机控制与平均场交互及共同噪声视角下的马尔可夫决策过程，构建了从多智能体强化学习到平均场控制的桥梁，涵盖概率、数学和控制理论框架，包括动态规划、混沌传播极限、表格Q学习和策略梯度方法的理论分析，以及表格方案和深度确定性策略梯度等数值实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维大规模智能体系统中，传统多智能体强化学习面临维度灾难和可扩展性问题。作者旨在建立平均场控制理论与强化学习方法之间的桥梁，为大规模随机群体设计可处理的、数学结构清晰的学习方法。

Method: 从多智能体强化学习与平均场控制的联系出发，构建了代表性智能体学习问题的概率、数学和控制理论框架；采用动态规划原理、混沌传播极限分析、表格Q学习与策略梯度方法的理论分析，以及数值实现包括表格方案和深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG）等深度强化学习方法。

Result: 建立了平均场强化学习的完整理论框架，涵盖一般模型和线性二次型模型的理论分析，并提供了表格型算法和深度强化学习方法的数值实现方案。

Conclusion: 该专著为平均场控制理论与强化学习方法论提供了连贯的桥梁，强调了问题的数学结构以及为大规模随机群体设计可处理学习方法的思路。

Abstract: This monograph provides an introduction to mean field reinforcement learning through the lens of Markov decision processes arising from large-population stochastic control with mean field interactions and common noise. Starting from the connection between multi-agent reinforcement learning and mean field control, it develops the probabilistic, mathematical, and control-theoretic framework needed to formulate representative-agent learning problems, analyze their relationship with finite-population systems, and study both general and linear-quadratic models. The presentation includes dynamic programming principles, propagation-of-chaos limits, and theoretical analyses of tabular Q-learning and policy-gradient methods. It also discusses numerical implementations, including tabular schemes and deep reinforcement learning methods such as deep deterministic policy gradient. The goal is to give readers a coherent bridge between mean field control theory and reinforcement learning methodology, emphasizing the mathematical structure of the problems and the design of tractable learning approaches for large stochastic populations.

</details>


### [126] [Symbolic Discovery of Iterative Algorithms: A Continuous Latent Space Bayesian Optimization Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01552)
*Tongjia Liu,Ilias Mitrai*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出使用潜在空间贝叶斯优化自动发现迭代优化算法，通过变分自编码器将离散更新函数空间映射为连续表示，再在此连续空间中进行贝叶斯优化搜索，在两个案例中成功发现符号形式的更新函数，且计算时间低于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动发现迭代优化算法的现有方法通常对更新函数的函数形式有预先假设，且基于数学规划的方法计算开销大。本文旨在克服这些局限，实现无需预设函数形式的自动化算法发现。

Method: 将算法发现建模为离散优化问题；使用变分自编码器（VAE）学习离散更新函数空间的连续表示；在得到的连续潜在空间中使用贝叶斯优化进行搜索，从而发现新的更新函数。

Result: 在两个案例研究中，该方法成功以符号形式发现了新的更新函数，且计算时间低于基于数学规划的现有方法。

Conclusion: 提出的方法能够自动发现迭代优化算法，无需对更新函数的函数形式做任何先验假设，且计算效率优于传统数学规划方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the automated discovery of iterative optimization algorithms. We formulate the algorithm discovery task as a discrete optimization problem and search for new update functions using latent space Bayesian Optimization. The proposed framework first learns a continuous representation of the discrete space of update functions using variational autoencoders, transforming the algorithm discovery task from a discrete to a continuous search problem. The continuous representation is subsequently used to search for new algorithms using Bayesian optimization. Application to two case studies shows that the proposed approach can discover new update functions in symbolic form without any assumptions on the functional form of the update function. Moreover, the computational time required to discover the new update functions is lower than existing mathematical programming-based approaches.

</details>


### [127] [Multi-Domain Matrix Framework for Human Resource Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01613)
*Daehan Lee,Jaemyung Ahn,Jaewoo Kim*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多域矩阵（MDM）的、面向小企业和初创公司的可操作人力资源决策支持框架，通过将成员、技能和项目视为相互关联的域，提供定性分析指南和定量指标，用于诊断组织HR状态并支持工作量重新分配、招聘和能力开发等决策。案例研究验证了该框架在早期科技初创公司中的实用性，能够识别工作量不平衡、揭示关键成员不可持续的工作量并指导后续招聘决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小企业和初创公司在HR决策中面临三大挑战：组织组件间复杂的相互依赖关系、缺乏系统性分析工具、以及快速变化环境中需要快速响应。现有HR管理方法缺乏针对这些挑战的结构化分析工具。

Method: 将初创企业人力资源管理建模为一个多域结构建模问题，使用多域矩阵（MDM）将成员、技能和项目作为相互关联的域进行整合；基于该表示提供定性分析指南和定量指标，用于诊断HR状态并支持人员决策。

Result: 案例研究表明，该框架能够识别工作量不平衡、揭示关键成员不可持续的工作量，并为后续招聘决策提供依据；招聘新成员后，框架可继续用于跟踪组织多域结构变化并支持持续HR诊断。

Conclusion: 该MDM-based HR决策支持框架为小企业和初创公司提供了一种系统化、可操作的工具，能够应对复杂依赖关系、缺乏分析工具和快速响应需求三大挑战，具有实际应用价值和持续诊断能力。

Abstract: This paper presents an actionable human resource (HR) decision-support framework for small firms and startups based on a multi-domain matrix (MDM). The framework addresses three key challenges faced by small organizations: complex interdependencies among organizational components; the lack of systematic analytical tools for HR decision-making; and the need for rapid responses in fast-changing organizational environments. The proposed framework formulates startup human resource management as a multi-domain structural modeling problem, where members, skills, and projects are interconnected domains within an integrated MDM. Based on this representation, the framework provides qualitative analysis guidelines and quantitative metrics for diagnosing an organization's HR state and supporting personnel decisions on workload redistribution, hiring, and capability development. A case study of MDM-based HR decisions for an early-stage technology startup is conducted to demonstrate the framework's practical applicability. The application shows that the framework can identify workload imbalances, reveal a key member with an unsustainable workload, and inform a subsequent hiring decision. The framework can be further applied after the hiring of a new member to track changes in the organization's multi-domain structure and support continuous HR diagnosis.

</details>


### [128] [Decentralized Stochastic Subgradient-type Methods with Communication Compression for Nonsmooth Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01755)
*Siyuan Zhang,Nachuan Xiao,Xin Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非光滑非凸去中心化优化问题，在通信压缩条件下，提出了一个统一框架，将多种去中心化随机次梯度方法纳入其中，包括无偏压缩和带误差补偿的收缩压缩。通过将一致性误差和平均迭代映射到连续时间微分包含的轨迹，建立了目标函数非光滑且缺乏Clarke正则性时的全局收敛性。基于该框架，进一步开发了基于符号正则化和梯度追踪动量的去中心化随机次梯度方法，数值实验验证了理论结果并揭示了通信-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化优化方法大多假设目标函数光滑或至少具有Clarke正则性，且通信压缩条件下的非光滑非凸问题缺乏统一的收敛性分析框架。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为各类压缩通信策略下的次梯度方法提供全局收敛保障。

Method: 提出通用框架统一无偏压缩和带误差补偿的收缩压缩两类方法；利用微分包含（differential inclusions）的连续时间轨迹分析一致性误差和平均迭代的动态行为；在该框架下设计了基于符号正则化（sign-based regularization）和梯度追踪动量的新方法。

Result: 建立了框架内所有方法在非光滑且缺乏Clarke正则性目标下的全局收敛性；新提出的基于符号正则化和梯度追踪动量的压缩方法在数值实验中有效，并展示了通信精度之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 本文提出的统一框架为通信压缩下的非光滑非凸去中心化优化提供了理论基石，所开发的新方法在实践中具有良好的通信-精度权衡表现，为大规模分布式系统的优化算法设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the nonsmooth nonconvex decentralized optimization problem, where inter-agent communication is compressed. We propose a general framework that unifies various decentralized stochastic subgradient-type methods with unbiased compression and contractive compression with error compensation. By relating the consensus-error iterates and the averaged iterates to the trajectories of continuous-time differential inclusions, we establish global convergence for all methods encompassed by our framework when the objective functions are nonsmooth and lack Clarke regularity. Based on our framework, we further develop several compression-based methods, including decentralized stochastic subgradient methods utilizing sign-based regularization and gradient-tracking momentum. Preliminary numerical experiments empirically support our theoretical results and highlight the communication-accuracy trade-off of the newly developed methods.

</details>


### [129] [Cognitive Warfare, Hybrid Pressure, and Sovereign Resilience: An Operations Research Framework Applied to the Iranian Case (2017--2026)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01894)
*Rahimeh Neamatian Monemi,Shahin Gelareh,Nelson Maculan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个耦合动力系统模型，将 grievance（不满情绪）和 institutional resilience（制度韧性）作为连续变量，与对手通过马尔可夫决策过程优化的压力强度相结合，证明均衡的存在性与局部稳定性，并通过伊朗认知战案例（2017-2026）和30个随机网络实例进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运筹学中的攻防模型将防御方状态视为离散分配而非连续、缓慢变化的制度过程，无法有效应对对手持续重新优化经济、媒体和心理压力的场景。

Method: 构建 grievance 与 institutional resilience 连续演化的耦合动力系统，压力强度由优化马尔可夫决策过程选择；证明均衡存在性与局部稳定性；通过30个随机网络实例、完整值迭代以及伊朗认知战案例（2017-2026）进行验证。

Result: 历史校准的伊朗案例位于计算出的运行崩溃边界约25倍以上；贪婪播种策略在随机实例中达到87%的平均网络渗透率，显著高于度中心性基线。

Conclusion: 从业者可通过均衡与边界计算评估具体案例相对于崩溃边界的位置，而非依赖未经验证的压力强度对比。

Abstract: A defending state facing sustained economic, media, and psychological pressure from an adversary that continuously re-optimises its campaign poses a problem that existing attacker-defender models in operations research do not directly resolve, because they treat the defender's state as a discrete allocation rather than a continuous, slow-moving institutional process. We formulate a coupled dynamical system in which grievance and institutional resilience evolve continuously while pressure intensity is chosen by an optimising Markov decision process, prove existence and local stability of the resulting equilibrium, and prove a formal result distinguishing it from standard feedback-stability analysis and from a stationary Markov chain treated in isolation. We validate the framework computationally using thirty randomised network instances, full value iteration, and a documented case study of cognitive warfare directed at Iran (2017--2026). The historically calibrated case sits approximately twenty-five times above the computed operational collapse boundary, and a greedy seeding policy reaches eighty-seven percent average network penetration across the randomised instances, significantly above a degree-centrality baseline. A practitioner can use the equilibrium and boundary computation to assess where a specific case sits relative to collapse, rather than relying on an unverified comparison between opposing pressure intensities.

</details>


### [130] [Lower Bounds for Anytime Acceleration of Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02053)
*Chung-En Tsai,Ilyas Fatkhullin,Liang Zhang,Niao He*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了梯度下降法在任意时刻收敛（anytime convergence）场景下的下界。作者证明了：对于函数值最小化，不存在任何正步长调度能实现优于 $o(n^{-1.334})$ 的任意时刻收敛率；对于平方梯度范数最小化，则不存在优于 $o(n^{-1})$ 的任意时刻收敛率。这是对COLT 2024开放问题的首次下界回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平滑凸优化中，GD使用大步长可在已知总迭代次数时达到 $O(n^{-1.271})$ 的收敛率，但在任意时刻收敛（迭代次数未知）场景下，已知最好上界仅为 $O(n^{-1.119})$（函数值）和 $O(n^{-1})$（梯度范数平方），远低于经典下界 $\Omega(n^{-2})$。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立任意时刻收敛的下界。

Method: 通过对二次函数和Huber函数的变体进行GD分析，推导出大步长步数的数量和大小的新上界，从而建立任意时刻收敛的下界。

Result: 建立了两个下界：(1) 函数值最小化：无正步长调度能达到 $o(n^{-1.334})$ 的任意时刻收敛率；(2) 平方梯度范数最小化：无正步长调度能达到 $o(n^{-1})$ 的任意时刻收敛率。

Conclusion: 本文首次回答了Kornowski和Shamir在COLT 2024提出的关于GD最优任意时刻收敛率的开放问题，给出了下界结果。

Abstract: Recent work suggests that the convergence rate of gradient descent (GD) in smooth convex optimization can be significantly improved by employing large stepsizes that may violate the descent property. In particular, if the total number of iterations $n$ is given, an $O(n^{-1.271})$ convergence rate can be achieved for both function value and squared gradient norm minimization. On the other hand, in the setting of anytime convergence, where $n$ is not known in advance, the best known rates of GD are much slower: $O(n^{-1.119})$ for function value minimization and $O(n^{-1})$ for squared gradient norm minimization. It remains open whether any of these upper bounds can be improved, as they are far from the classical $Ω(n^{-2})$ lower bound for any first-order method.
  In this work, we establish two lower bounds on the anytime convergence of GD. We show that no positive stepsize schedule can achieve an $o(n^{-1.334})$ anytime rate for function value minimization, nor an $o(n^{-1})$ anytime rate for squared gradient norm minimization. The key ingredients of our analysis are novel upper bounds on the number and the magnitude of large stepsizes, derived by analyzing GD on quadratic functions and variants of Huber functions. Our work provides the first lower bounds for the COLT 2024 open problem posed by Kornowski and Shamir regarding the optimal anytime convergence rates of GD.

</details>


### [131] [Invariance Entropy in the Dust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02279)
*Senhan Yao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 通过单一构造否定了Kawan关于控制系统中不变熵的两个长期开放问题：有限严格不变熵与普通不变熵未必相等，且严格不变熵在Hausdorff扰动下未必下半连续。构造利用Cantor坐标存储无限符号指令、指数收缩坐标隐藏晚期不匹配、紧致匹配图强制精确符号一致，揭示了由精确生存约束持久性和不变熵中极限顺序产生的信息复杂性新来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Kawan关于控制系统不变熵的两个自然推广问题（已开放超过15年）：有限严格不变熵是否等于普通不变熵，以及严格不变熵在Hausdorff扰动下是否下半连续。

Method: 构建一个连续时间控制系统，包含三个关键组件：Cantor坐标存储无限符号指令、指数收缩坐标使晚期不匹配几何上不可见、紧致匹配图强制精确符号一致。通过单一构造同时否定两个猜想。

Result: 有限严格不变熵不必等于普通不变熵；严格不变熵在Hausdorff扰动下不必下半连续。

Conclusion: 识别出一种信息复杂性的新来源，它并非由动力学扩张产生，而是由精确生存约束在薄不变几何下的持久性以及不变熵中极限的顺序所驱动。

Abstract: We answer negatively two natural general forms of Kawan's questions on invariance entropy for control systems, open for more than fifteen years, by a single construction. We show that finite strict invariance entropy need not coincide with ordinary invariance entropy, and that strict invariance entropy need not be lower semicontinuous under Hausdorff perturbations of the initial set. The construction is a continuous-time control system in which a Cantor coordinate stores an infinite symbolic instruction, an exponentially contracting coordinate makes late mismatches geometrically invisible, and a compact matching graph forces exact symbolic agreement. It identifies a source of information complexity not generated by dynamical expansion, but by the persistence of exact viability constraints under thin invariant geometry and by the order of limits in invariance entropy.

</details>


### [132] [Sensitivity Analysis and Robust Optimal Control for Coupled Evolution Inclusions with State-Dependent Maximal Monotone Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02339)
*Jinsheng Du,Boris Mordukhovich,Shengda Zeng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类由半线性演化包含和状态依赖极大单调算子控制的强耦合非光滑系统，建立了参数化解映射的正则性理论，证明了Bolza型优化问题解的存在性与值函数连续性，分析了多种参数不确定下的控制框架（固定参数、协同设计、min-max鲁棒控制和Hurwicz折中控制），并通过数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决强耦合非光滑动力系统中参数化优化与控制的理论空白，特别是当系统同时包含半线性演化包含和状态依赖极大单调算子时，现有理论无法直接处理此类复杂耦合结构下的优化问题。

Method: 首先建立参数化解映射的适定性、紧性及Painlevé-Kuratowski连续性；然后利用变分分析和集值分析工具证明Bolza型优化问题的最优存在性、值函数连续性及最优解映射的上半连续性；进而将理论推广至固定参数控制、协同设计、min-max鲁棒控制和Hurwicz折中控制四种框架；最后通过数值实验验证理论预测的敏感性与鲁棒性。

Result: 建立了参数化解映射的正则性理论；证明了Bolza型优化问题最优对的存在性及值函数的连续性；获得了四种不同控制框架下的存在性结果；数值实验证实了理论预测的敏感性与鲁棒性现象。

Conclusion: 本文为强耦合非光滑系统提供了完整的优化与控制理论基础，所建立的框架可有效处理含参数不确定性的复杂动力系统优化问题，数值结果验证了理论的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: We consider a class of strongly coupled nonsmooth systems consisting of a semilinear evolution inclusion and a differential inclusion governed by state-dependent maximal monotone operators. Our main contributions are fourfold. First, we collect the well-posedness, compactness, and Painlevé--Kuratowski continuity properties of the parameterized solution map required for the subsequent optimization analysis. Second, for Bolza-type optimization over the solution set, we prove the existence of optimal pairs, establish continuity properties of the value function, and derive upper semicontinuity of the optimal-solution map. Third, we study fixed-parameter optimal control, simultaneous control-parameter design, min--max robust control, and Hurwicz-type compromise control under parameter uncertainty, and we establish existence results for each formulation. Fourth, we report numerical experiments for sweeping-type systems that illustrate the sensitivity and robustness phenomena predicted by the theory.

</details>


<div id='stat.AP'></div>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Sampling for Region-Aggregated Spatial Scan Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01451)
*Foad Namjoo,Drew McClelland,Michael Matheny,Jeff M. Phillips*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 针对地理空间数据异常检测中区域聚合数据与点基扫描统计不匹配的问题，提出将每个区域均匀采样20-50个点并均分其值的方法，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升统计功效，建议作为默认转换方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的空间扫描统计方法多基于点数据，而地理空间数据通常按预先定义的区域（如人口普查区、邮政编码区、县）聚合。标准做法是将每个区域简化为其质心（如SaTScan工具），但这种方法丢弃了区域的空间范围信息，导致统计功效显著损失。因此需要一种既能保留区域空间信息、又能兼容高效点基扫描算法的方法。

Method: 提出基于采样的转换方法：将每个空间区域均匀采样20-50个点，并将该区域的值均匀分配（spread evenly）到这些采样点上。通过这种方式，区域的空间范围信息得以保留，同时生成的点数据可无缝接入现有的点基空间扫描统计算法。

Result: 该方法在提升统计功效方面表现显著，同时保持了计算上的可处理性。收敛性分析解释了为什么每个区域仅需20-50个样本点就足以达到良好效果。

Conclusion: 建议将这种基于采样的转换方法作为将点基空间扫描统计应用于区域聚合数据的默认方式，以提升异常检测的统计效能。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in geospatial data is a crucial tool in geographic information science (GIS), with applications ranging from national security to public-health surveillance to the study of societal disparities. This work focuses on spatial scan statistics and addresses a key mismatch: spatial counts are typically aggregated into predefined regions (census tracts, zip codes, counties), whereas the most efficient scan algorithms operate on spatial point data. The standard remedy -- collapsing each region to its centroid, as in widely used tools such as SaTScan -- is convenient but, as we show, discards the region's spatial extent and causes a significant loss in statistical power. To resolve this, we propose a simple yet scalable fix: replace each spatial region with 20-50 points sampled uniformly from its geometry and spread the region's values evenly across them. This approach improves statistical power while maintaining computational tractability. A convergence analysis explains why so few samples per region suffice. We recommend this sampling-based conversion as the default way to apply point-based spatial scan statistics to region-aggregated data for anomaly detection.

</details>


### [134] [Sensitivity Analysis and Optimization of Stochastic Epidemic Models under Parameter Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01476)
*Nicholas R. Wu,Michael C. Fu*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对离散时间随机流行病模型，推导出无偏梯度估计量，用于敏感性分析和优化，并发现参数不确定性会降低对目标参数的敏感性，导致更保守的干预策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流行病模型的敏感性分析通常基于确定性极限近似（如大种群假设），忽略了有限种群、有限时间范围和参数不确定性的影响。本文旨在推导无偏梯度估计量，以更准确地评估参数不确定性下的模型敏感度，并指导优化决策。

Method: 针对离散时间随机流行病模型，推导无偏梯度估计量，估计总感染数对免疫比例（v）和接触率（β）的敏感性。与基于大种群近似的确定性极限估计量进行比较，并通过仿真实验评估参数不确定性对敏感性和优化策略的影响。

Result: 所提估计量对β的导数方差低于有限差分估计量，但对v的导数方差更高。参数不确定性降低了模型对目标参数的敏感性，尤其是疫苗的间接效应（如群体免疫）在参数不确定时减弱。优化问题中，考虑参数不确定性会导致更保守的策略。

Conclusion: 在随机流行病模型的敏感性分析和优化中，纳入参数不确定性至关重要，忽略它可能高估干预措施的效果（如疫苗接种）并导致非保守策略。

Abstract: To address sensitivity analysis and optimization for a discrete-time stochastic epidemic model, we derive unbiased gradient estimators that accommodate uncertainties represented as distributions over the parameters of interest, such as those arising from Bayesian calibration.
  Specifically, we estimate the sensitivity of total infections over a finite time horizon with respect to the proportion immunized ($v$) and the contact rate ($β$). Comparing the proposed estimators with deterministic limit approximations based on large populations reveals differences due to the finite population and time horizon. The estimators exhibit lower variance than finite-difference estimators for the derivative with respect to $β$, but higher variance for the derivative with respect to $v$. Simulation experiments indicate parameter uncertainty reduces sensitivity to the parameters of interest. In particular, indirect effects of vaccination, such as herd immunity, are less pronounced compared to when parameters are known. For optimization problems balancing intervention and infection costs, incorporating parametric uncertainty leads to more conservative policies.

</details>


### [135] [An unsupervised kernel norm monitoring for fault detection in a time series photovoltaic system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01541)
*Victoria Jorry,Zina-Sabrina Duma,Satu-Pia Reinikainen,Heikki Haario,Lassi Roininen*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出核范数监测（KNM），一种基于核方法的无监督滑窗故障检测方法，在光伏并网系统中对传感器故障和局部阴影两类故障分别达到99.1%和98.3%的准确率，显著优于OCSVM、iForest和LOF等基线方法，且具有可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际并网光伏系统中故障标签数据稀缺，监督方法难以应用；现有无监督方法（如OCSVM、iForest、LOF）虽能捕捉非线性行为，但其核超参数调优需要故障先验知识，限制了实用性。本文旨在提出一种无需先验故障知识、可自适应调参的无监督故障检测方法。

Method: 提出核范数监测（KNM），一种基于核方法的无监督滑窗故障检测方法。该方法在正常数据上训练，通过滑窗计算核范数作为异常评分；采用Cauchy核等核函数以灵活塑造决策边界，避免了传统方法需先验知识调参的局限性。同时提出变量贡献图以支持故障识别。

Result: 在GPVS-Faults数据集（IPPT模式）上，KNM在传感器故障场景准确率达99.1%，局部阴影场景达98.3%；最佳基线方法（OCSVM等）最高仅93.5%。Cauchy核表现最优。

Conclusion: KNM是一种通用型无监督工业过程监测框架，在无标签数据条件下显著优于现有无监督基线方法，且具备可解释性，适用于光伏并网系统及其他工业过程的故障检测。

Abstract: Grid-connected photovoltaic systems (GCPVS) are generally robust but remain susceptible to faults that can compromise energy conversion efficiency or raise safety concerns. Promptly and automatically detecting such anomalies is therefore essential for maintaining system reliability and performance. However, in practice, labeled fault data are rarely available in real-world deployments, which limits the applicability of supervised approaches. Conventional unsupervised baseline models, including a one-class support vector machine (OCSVM), isolation forest (iForest), and local outlier factor (LOF), are trained on normal operation data and assign anomaly scores reflecting how closely new observations resemble that baseline. Although these methods already accommodate non-linear behavior to varying degrees, kernel-based formulations offer further flexibility in shaping the decision boundary; however, tuning the kernel hyperparameters ordinarily requires some prior knowledge of the fault regime. We overcome this limitation by proposing kernel-based norm monitoring (KNM), a non-linear, unsupervised, window-based fault-detection method designed for continuous processes. Although the paper focuses on the GCPVS as a case study, KNM is a general-purpose monitoring framework applicable to a wide range of industrial processes. Using the Grid-connected PV System Faults (GPVS-Faults) dataset operating in intermediate power point tracking (IPPT) mode, KNM is evaluated in two fault scenarios, sensor faults and partial shading, against three benchmark techniques: OCSVM, iForest, and LOF. KNM achieves up to 99.1% and 98.3% accuracy on the two fault scenarios, respectively, using the Cauchy kernel, compared to 93.5% for the best-performing benchmark. The method is interpretable, and variable contribution plots are proposed to support fault identification.

</details>


### [136] [An Adaptive Glicko-2 Rating Framework for Probabilistic Football Forecasting and Season Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01722)
*Bich Van Nguyen,Nam Anh Tran*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自适应Glicko-2的足球比赛结果预测与联赛赛季模拟框架，通过引入胜分差调整、 dominance加权、结构性冲击、主场优势建模和有序逻辑回归平局模型等足球特定机制，实现动态球队实力估计和概率性预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Elo等评分系统虽简洁可解释，但未显式建模不确定性且忽略了足球比赛的特定上下文信息（如平局的重要性、球队实力随时间变化等），因此需要一种更适应足球特点的动态评分与预测方法。

Method: 基于Glicko-2评分系统进行扩展，融入五项足球特定机制：胜分差调整、 dominance加权、结构性冲击、主场优势建模及有序逻辑回归平局模型；通过蒙特卡洛模拟对联赛剩余赛季进行采样。

Result: 隐式动态估计球队实力，将评分差异转化为胜-平-负概率分布，并用于联赛赛季的剩余赛程概率模拟。

Conclusion: 该框架相较于传统Elo系统能更好地处理足球预测中的不确定性、平局结构和动态实力变化，为足球比赛的概率性预测和赛季模拟提供了一种系统化方案。

Abstract: Football match outcome prediction is a challenging problem because team strength changes over time, match outcomes contain a high level of randomness, and draws play a central role in the result structure. Classical rating systems such as Elo provide simple and interpretable dynamic summaries of team ability, but they do not explicitly model uncertainty and often ignore football-specific contextual information. This paper proposes an adaptive Glicko-2-based rating framework for probabilistic football forecasting and leaguelevel season simulation. The proposed framework extends the standard Glicko-2 model by incorporating football-specific mechanisms, including margin-of-victory adjustment, dominance weighting, structural shocks, home advantage modelling, and an ordered-logit draw model. The framework estimates latent team strength dynamically, converts rating differences into win-draw-loss probabilities, and uses these probabilities to simulate the remaining part of a league season through Monte Carlo sampling.

</details>


### [137] [Inverse Suitability: Identifying Condition Difficulty and Rider Skill from Behavioural Outcomes via Continuous-Item Response Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01961)
*Fabio Carucci*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出逆适宜性模型（Inverse Suitability），基于项目反应理论（IRT），从行为结果中分离出户外活动（风筝冲浪、滑翔伞、滑雪）的环境难度与个人技能，并通过合成数据验证了模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有户外活动适宜性评分将环境条件的固有难度与个人技能混淆在单一专家曲线中，无法独立测量这两个关键因素。作者希望从行为结果中解耦出技能与难度，构建更通用的量化框架。

Method: 采用连续型项目反应理论（IRT）模型，定义P(y=1) = sigma(a(theta_r - delta(x, s)))，其中theta_r为潜在骑手技能，delta(x,s)为以物理专家曲线为先验的潜在难度函数，a为区分度参数。参数通过边际最大似然估计与Gauss-Hermite求积法估计。

Result: 在80骑手×30结果的参考队列上，模型恢复潜在技能的相关性r=0.96，难度最小值定位误差在3个单位内，留出法Brier Skill Score比专家曲线基线提升+0.33。

Conclusion: 恢复的难度函数可构建可测量的场地级固有难度图谱，这是现有气象观测网络无法捕获的。模型在合成数据上可单命令复现，无需专有观测数据。

Abstract: Suitability scoring for outdoor activities (kitesurfing, paragliding, ski touring) maps environmental conditions to a go/no-go verdict via expert-defined curves. These curves conflate two distinct quantities: the intrinsic difficulty of a condition and the skill of the person facing it. We introduce Inverse Suitability, a continuous-item Item Response Theory (IRT) model that identifies both from behavioural outcomes alone. Each outcome is a triple (rider r, condition metric x at site s, binary outcome y); we model P(y=1) = sigma(a (theta_r - delta(x, s))), where theta_r is latent rider skill, delta(x, s) is a latent difficulty function anchored to a physics-derived expert curve as its prior, and a is a discrimination parameter. The formulation is strictly more general than a single suitability curve, which it recovers exactly when skill is integrated out under the population distribution. Parameters are estimated by marginal maximum likelihood with Gauss-Hermite quadrature; identification holds when the rider-by-condition incidence graph is connected, with a documented single-curve fallback otherwise. We validate via synthetic recovery: on a reference cohort (80 riders times 30 outcomes) the model recovers latent skill at r = 0.96, locates the difficulty minimum within 3 units of ground truth, and improves held-out Brier Skill Score by +0.33 over the expert-curve baseline. The recovered difficulty function defines a measurable, site-level construct, an intrinsic difficulty atlas, that existing meteorological observation networks do not capture. All results reproduce from a single command on synthetic data, requiring no proprietary observations.

</details>


### [138] [Convergence fragility in probit Bayesian kernel machine regression implemented in the bkmr R package for binary-outcome environmental mixture analyses: a simulation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02000)
*Akifumi Eguchi,Takayuki Kawashima,Tomotaka Momozaki,Tomoyuki Nakagawa*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文评估了贝叶斯核机器回归（BKMR）probit流程的MCMC收敛性，发现431个预定义任务中仅30个（7%）满足R-hat≤1.01且ESS≥400的收敛标准，表明拟合完成不等同于收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BKMR在二元结局暴露混合物分析中被广泛使用，但之前的模拟研究未区分执行成功与MCMC收敛诊断，可能高估了方法有效性。

Method: 使用bkmr包的SimData()生成数据、kmbayes()拟合模型，设置四条链（随机初始化，种子20260621），评估R-hat、bulk-ESS和tail-ESS等收敛诊断指标。

Result: 431个任务中430个返回拟合对象（1个数值未完成）；仅55个任务R-hat≤1.01，85个bulk-ESS≥400，44个tail-ESS≥400；同时满足三个标准的仅30个（7%）。

Conclusion: BKMR probit拟合完成不代表MCMC收敛，实际分析应报告链数、预热/保留迭代数、R-hat、bulk-ESS和tail-ESS，而非仅依赖固定迭代次数或拟合完成状态。

Abstract: Background. Bayesian kernel machine regression (BKMR) is widely used for exposure-mixture analyses with binary outcomes through a probit extension. Because a bkmr fit can complete without providing adequate effective posterior information, simulation studies should separate execution success from MCMC convergence diagnostics. Methods. We evaluated the public bkmr probit workflow using bkmr::SimData() for data generation, bkmr::kmbayes() for model fitting, and posterior for convergence diagnostics. The balanced generator used family = "binomial", hfun = 2, beta.true = 0.5, ind = 1:2, and M = 4. SimData() generated the covariate as X = 3*cos(z1) + 2*rnorm(n). Four chains were initialized with chain-specific randomized starting values generated reproducibly from the fixed initial-value base seed 20260621. These values affected only the initial state of the sampler and did not alter the BKMR model, default priors, or Metropolis-Hastings proposals. Results. Of 431 prespecified tasks, 430 returned fitted objects and one task had a numerical non-completion. Diagnostic adequacy was limited: rank-normalized R-hat <= 1.01 threshold was achieved in 55/431 tasks, bulk-ESS >= 400 in 85/431, tail-ESS >= 400 in 44/431, and both ESS criteria in 44/431. The primary diagnostic criterion, R-hat at or below the 1.01 threshold with both bulk-ESS and tail-ESS >= 400, was met in 30/431 prespecified tasks, corresponding to 30/430 completed fits. Conclusions. Completion of probit BKMR fits in bkmr should not be equated with convergence of the retained MCMC draws. Applied analyses should report the number of chains, warmup and retained iterations, rank-normalized R-hat, bulk-ESS, and tail-ESS rather than rely on a fixed iteration count or on fit completion alone.

</details>


### [139] [Quaternion Nondecimated Wavelet Descriptors for Multiclass Breast Histology Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02133)
*Sara Antonijevic,Brani Vidakovic*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于四元数非抽取小波变换的乳腺癌组织学分类可解释框架，将RGB图像编码为纯四元数场，提取多尺度、方向性、颜色耦合的特征进行四类（正常、良性、原位、浸润）组织分类，无需预训练网络或外部数据库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将RGB通道转为灰度或作为独立标量图像处理，丢失了H&E染色图像中固有的彩色诊断信息。需要一种能保持颜色作为单一向量量、同时捕捉多尺度和方向性信息的可解释方法。

Method: 将RGB图像编码为纯四元数场，应用二维四元数非抽取小波变换（QNDWT2D）生成多尺度、方向性、颜色耦合的系数场。从这些系数中构建可解释的特征族，包括染色彩平衡、小波能量、幅度异质性、四元数相位集中度、颜色轴几何、方向各向异性、方向熵和尺度依赖的能量衰减。使用径向核SVM在BACH数据集上评估，采用重复嵌套交叉验证。

Result: 在BACH四类组织分类中实现平衡的识别准确率，错误主要发生在相邻类别之间，正常与浸润极少混淆。基于排列重要性分析，方向性、相位集中度、各向异性、尺度和幅度变异性等特征组均对分类有贡献，表明分类器利用了真实的四元数和多尺度几何信息。

Conclusion: 该框架无需预训练网络、学习滤波器或外部数据库，提供了一个可复现、可解释的计算病理学基线方法。

Abstract: Breast histology images carry diagnostic information in color, texture, orientation, and tissue architecture across a range of scales. In H&E microscopy this information is inherently chromatic and is not fully recovered when the red, green, and blue (RGB) channels are reduced to grayscale or transformed as independent scalar images. We propose an interpretable quaternion nondecimated wavelet framework for breast histology classification. Each RGB image is encoded as a pure quaternion field, and a quaternion nondecimated wavelet transform in two dimensions (QNDWT2D) produces multiscale, directional, color-coupled coefficient fields on the original image grid, keeping color as a single vector quantity rather than three separate channels. From these coefficients we build interpretable feature families summarizing stain balance, wavelet energy, amplitude heterogeneity, quaternion phase concentration, color-axis geometry, directional anisotropy, orientation entropy, and scale-dependent energy decay, each tied to a histopathological property such as nuclear density or glandular organization. We evaluate the descriptors on the BreAst Cancer Histology (BACH) challenge, a balanced four-class set of normal, benign, in situ, and invasive tissue, using a radial-kernel support vector machine (SVM) with repeated nested cross-validation. The descriptors yield balanced recognition across classes, with errors concentrated among adjacent categories while normal and invasive are rarely reversed. Permutation importance shows that directional, phase-concentration, anisotropy, scale, and amplitude-variability groups all contribute, indicating that the classifier draws on genuine quaternion and multiscale geometry rather than global color alone. The framework uses no pretrained networks, learned filters, or external databases, offering a reproducible, interpretable baseline for computational pathology.

</details>


### [140] [Value-of-Information Analysis for External Validation of Risk Prediction Models in Multicenter Studies and Systematic Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02321)
*Laure Wynants,Kim Zhipei Wang,Sabine Grimm,Andrea Gabrio,Andrew Vickers,Ewout Steyerberg,Ben Van Calster,Mohsen Sadatsafavi*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文扩展了期望完美信息价值（EVPI）方法，考虑多中心研究和meta分析中的中心间异质性，区分全局与局部最优策略、观测与未观测中心，定义了EVPIglobal、EVPIcluster_j、EVPIcluster及EVPPIcluster,prevalence，并通过ADNEX模型在36个中心的系统评价进行实证，证明该方法能量化不确定性来源并判断是否需要额外验证研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EVPI方法仅聚焦单中心研究，忽略了多中心研究和meta分析中存在的中心间异质性，导致无法准确量化预测模型净获益相对于默认策略的不确定性。

Method: 将EVPI和EVPPI扩展至多中心场景，区分全局最优策略与局部最优策略、观测中心与未观测中心，并定义四类指标：EVPIglobal、EVPIcluster_j、EVPIcluster、EVPPIcluster,prevalence，通过MetaNB R包实现。

Result: ADNEX模型在36个中心的数据显示：全局决策下EVPIglobal为0，无需更多数据；观测中心局部EVPIcluster_j通常低于单独考虑局部数据的结果；未观测中心有0.03概率默认策略更优；消除所有中心不确定性每年可净避免1134个假阳性，仅确定局部患病率可净避免158个假阳性。

Conclusion: EVPI扩展方法能量化不同来源的不确定性及其对临床效用的影响，帮助判断是否需要额外验证研究以确定全局或局部最优策略，考虑跨中心异质性对临床效用的影响至关重要。

Abstract: External validation studies have finite sample sizes, creating uncertainty about whether a prediction model's Net Benefit (NB) exceeds default strategies' NB. The expected value of perfect information (EVPI) quantifies consequences of uncertainty. Current EVPI methods focus on single studies, ignoring between-center heterogeneity. We extend EVPI and expected value of partial perfect information (EVPPI) to account for between-cluster heterogeneity in multicenter studies and meta-analyses. We distinguish between the global and local optimal strategy and between observed and unobserved clusters. We define EVPIglobal, EVPIcluster_j, EVPIcluster, and EVPPIcluster,prevalence, implemented in the MetaNB R package, and illustrate them using a systematic review across 36 centers of the ADNEX model for ovarian cancer diagnosis. Assuming one global decision regarding ADNEX adoption, there is no need for further data to confirm ADNEX is superior overall (EVPIglobal 0). Meta-analysis borrows information across observed clusters, resulting in consistent local superiority of ADNEX and nonzero but typically lower EVPIcluster_j than when considering local data alone. There is 0.03 probability default strategies are superior in unobserved centers. Eliminating uncertainty on performance and prevalence in each (EVPIcluster) would gain 1134 net avoided false positives (FP) per year, assuming 350000 tumors annually with 20% malignancies. Determining only local prevalence with certainty (EVPPIcluster, prevalence) would gain net 158 avoided FP per year. EVPI extensions disentangle sources of uncertainty and quantify the need for further validation to determine the global or locally optimal strategy. Considering uncertainty and heterogeneity in clinical utility across clusters is essential to decide whether additional validation studies are warranted.

</details>


### [141] [Instrumented difference-in-differences under case-control sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02327)
*Tran Trong Khoi Le,Emilie Sbidian,Tat-Thang Vo*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对病例-对照设计提出了一种新的工具变量差分法（iDiD），该方法基于结构均值模型，允许工具变量存在时间不变的直接效应，并在回顾性抽样中有效处理选择偏差，从而在稀有结局研究中实现对未测量混杂的校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病例-对照设计中现有的工具变量方法依赖排他性约束假设，但该假设常被违反（即工具变量可能通过独立于暴露的路径直接影响结局），亟需一种能放宽该假设的稳健方法。

Method: 提出instrumented difference-in-differences（iDiD）方法，基于结构均值模型框架，允许IV候选者存在时间不变的直接效应；在回顾性病例-对照数据中，通过有效处理选择偏差，使得候选者仍可在趋势尺度上作为有效工具使用。

Result: 通过广泛模拟评估了有限样本性能，并应用于法国国家索赔数据库中银屑病生物治疗严重感染风险的实际数据分析。

Conclusion: iDiD方法为病例-对照设计中放宽排他性约束提供了可行方案，在存在时间不变直接效应的IV候选者场景下仍能获得有效的因果估计。

Abstract: Case-control designs are fundamental in epidemiology for the efficient study of rare outcomes. Although instrumental variable (IV) methods have been extended to this setting to address unmeasured confounding, they typically rely on the exclusion restriction assumption, which may be violated when the IV candidates directly affect the outcome through pathways independent of the exposure. In this paper, we propose a novel instrumented difference-in-differences (iDiD) approach tailored to case-control designs. Grounded in structural mean modeling, the proposed method accommodates IV candidates that have time-invariant direct effect on the outcome. When retrospective case-control datasets are collected, the candidate can still be used as a valid instrument on the trend scale when selection bias induced by retrospective sampling is efficiently taken into account. We assess finite-sample performance of this method through extensive simulations, then apply it to evaluate the risk of serious infection of biologic treatments for psoriasis, using French national claim database.

</details>


### [142] [Masked complex non-decimated wavelet features for patient-level classification of contrast-enhanced mammography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02394)
*Sara Antonijevic,Brani Vidakovic*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对对比增强光谱乳腺摄影（CESM）分类器构建中两个未解决的核心问题——两种图像类型（低能图像与对比增强图像）是否携带相当的恶性信号，以及患者多幅图像如何整合为单一决策——提出了一个基于掩蔽复非解小波特征库与弹性网络逻辑回归分类器的透明、无泄漏基线框架。在患者分组嵌套交叉验证评估下，两种图像在患者级AUC上无统计差异，但通过不同可解释通道编码恶性信息，为未来CESM分类器提供了可比较的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CESM分类器普遍存在数据泄漏问题（在图像级划分训练/测试集，导致同一患者图像跨集），夸大了报告性能；同时两个核心问题（两种图像类型的恶性信号可比性、多图融合策略）未能可靠解答。

Method: 采用掩蔽复非解小波特征库（masked complex non-decimated wavelet feature bank）结合弹性网络逻辑回归分类器；在CDD-CESM数据集（1880张图像，308名患者）上使用患者分组重复嵌套交叉验证及患者聚类bootstrap推断进行无泄漏评估。

Result: 两种图像在患者级AUC上无统计差异；对比增强图像单图融合患者级AUC为0.874（95%CI 0.827-0.918），低能图像与之统计无差异；两种图像通过不同可解释通道编码恶性信息（低能图像：相位相干性；对比图像：幅度分布）；所提框架在患者级匹配预训练ResNet-50性能。

Conclusion: 该框架为未来CESM分类器提供了一个透明、无泄漏的基线基准；同时证明了尽管两种图像类型在判别性能上无统计差异，但通过可解释的物理通道编码互补的恶性信息。

Abstract: Contrast-enhanced spectral mammography (CESM) acquires two images of each breast, a low-energy image and a recombined contrast image, but two questions central to building a classifier on them remain unsettled: whether the two image types carry comparable malignancy signal, and how a patient's several images should be combined into a single decision. Both are hard to answer reliably, because most published CESM classifiers split cross-validation folds at the image level, letting images of the same patient fall in both training and test sets and inflating reported performance. We pair a masked complex non-decimated wavelet feature bank with an elastic-net logistic classifier, evaluated under repeated patient-grouped nested cross-validation with patient-cluster bootstrap inference on the CDD-CESM dataset (1,880 images, 308 patients); under this leakage-free evaluation the inflation from testing on previously seen patients is negligible. On normal-versus-malignant detection, the two acquisitions are statistically indistinguishable in patient-level AUC under the proposed evaluation framework. Under single-image fusion the contrast image reaches a patient-level AUC of 0.874 (95% CI 0.827-0.918) and the low-energy image is statistically indistinguishable from it, yet the two encode malignancy through disjoint, interpretable channels: phase coherence on the low-energy image and magnitude distribution on the contrast image. The framework matches a pretrained ResNet-50 representation at the patient level, but whereas the frozen deep representation is not directly interpretable at the level of individual predictors, every predictor in the wavelet representation carries an explicit physical meaning. The result is a transparent, leakage-free baseline against which future CESM classifiers can be measured.

</details>
